Assign world art through the Collectible template's Art item field. Refresh restarts that pickup's timer; Extend adds its remaining time. Different power-ups run independently.

  1. Open Project ▸ Game Settings… in Defold.
  2. Select Equipment.
  3. Tick Power-ups in the Power-ups section.
  4. Click Save and Prepare & Check.
  5. Reopen MVTK.tiled-project in Tiled with File ▸ Open File or Project....
  6. Place a Collectible template on the top-level templates layer.
  7. Set Collectible type to powerup_speed or powerup_invincibility.
  8. Tick Persistence ▸ Respawns.
  9. Set Art item to an imported catalog image's name.
  10. Choose File ▸ Export Current Room.
  11. Run Project ▸ Prepare & Check in Defold.
  12. Choose Project ▸ Build.
  13. Open Layout Editor from the title screen.
  14. Click Export.
  15. Choose Play Game on an empty save slot.

Invincibility blocks ordinary enemy, projectile, contact and non-lethal hazard hits. With hazard returns on, pits return the player without damage. Kill-mode hazards, lethal hits, drowning and damage over time still apply. Speed affects walking and running only, including their air movement; swimming and climbing keep their own speeds. Timers freeze during pause and clear at death or a new player session. Persistence ▸ Respawns overrides the catalog default; keep it ticked so a pickup returns after its room unloads and reloads. With Power-ups off, it stays in place and Prepare & Check warns.

HUD and prompts ▸ Show on screen ▸ Power-ups controls the name/countdown rows. Check the HUD at 16:9 and 21:9: active rows move the minimap down and expired rows free the space again. Test speed on slopes and ice, then pause and die while a power-up is active.

Save slot cards, copy and delete#

Each save slot card on the Play Game screen shows the slot's region name with its colour from biomes.lua, then a line of summary fields, for example 63% 2h 14m Bosses 3. A save made before summaries existed, or one not yet saved in play, shows its play time and last-saved date instead. Players press X (or Delete) to delete a slot and C (or RB) to copy one onto another slot; both ask for a confirmation that starts on No. A copy keeps the replaced save in the target slot's backup.

To show or hide the cards' summaries and the copy action:

  1. Choose Project ▸ Game Settings….
  2. Click Saves and checkpoints.
  3. Tick or untick Save slots ▸ Slot summaries.
  4. Tick or untick Save slots ▸ Copy saves.
  5. Click Save and Prepare & Check.
  6. Choose Project ▸ Build.

To choose the summary fields and their order:

  1. Open developer/config/game_config.lua.
  2. Find summary_fields under progression.saves.
  3. List the fields you want, for example { "completion", "playtime", "bosses" }. Allowed fields: completion, playtime, bosses, abilities, level, ending and last_saved.
  4. Save game_config.lua.
  5. Choose Project ▸ Prepare & Check.
  6. Choose Project ▸ Build.
  7. Play a save slot and use a save point.
  8. Quit to the title screen.
  9. Choose Play Game.
Expected result: The slot's card shows its region and the listed fields in your order. A field the save has no value for is left out, and a New Game+ cycle above 0 joins the end of the line.

To try copy and delete:

  1. Choose Play Game on the title screen.
  2. Select a slot that holds a save.
  3. Press C, or RB on a controller.
  4. Press Down to choose the target slot.
  5. Press Enter, or the controller confirm button.
  6. Press Right to select Yes.
  7. Press Enter to copy.
  8. Select a slot that holds a save.
  9. Press X, or the left face button on a controller.
  10. Press Enter while No is selected.
Expected result: The target slot's card now matches the copied slot, and the slot you chose to delete still holds its save. Back cancels a copy before you confirm.

Offer New Game+#

New Game+ ships off. It offers another run on a completed save slot, with configurable carried progress and cycle difficulty. Do not test it inside a Dev Menu test session or boss practice.

  1. Choose Project ▸ Game Settings….
  2. Click Progression.
  3. Tick New Game+ ▸ Enabled.
  4. Set Enemy health multiplier for cycle 1.
  5. Set Damage taken multiplier for cycle 1.
  6. Set Carry level (applies while Leveling is on).
  7. Set Carry equipment.
  8. Set Carry currency.
  9. Click Save and Prepare & Check.
  10. Choose Project ▸ Build.
  11. Play an empty save slot to an ending.
  12. Return to the title screen.
  13. Choose Play Game.
  14. Select the completed slot.
  15. Press G, or the top face button on a controller, to open New Game+.
  16. Press Right to select Yes (the prompt starts on No).
  17. Press Enter, or the controller confirm button, to start the next cycle.

Choosing No or cancelling the prompt leaves the save unchanged. Yes rewrites the same slot after verifying a backup, then starts at the player start. Journal ▸ Records shows New Game+ and Cycle {cycle}. Quit and Continue keeps that cycle and carried progress. The slot's last-saved line also shows its cycle above 0.

Difficulty always carries. Default carry keeps equipment, wallet and bank currency, level and total experience, bestiary knowledge and seen cutscenes. Upgrades, abilities and other items reset by default. World state and run statistics reset. The cycle factors multiply the chosen difficulty.

For later cycles, allowed endings and additional carry rules:

  1. Open developer/config/new_game_plus.lua.
  2. Set endings to an allowed list of ending ids, or leave {} to accept any configured ending.
  3. Set the remaining carry switches for the progress the next run should retain.
  4. Set later entries in cycles; the final entry repeats for later runs.
  5. Save the file.
  6. Choose Project ▸ Reload Editor Scripts.
  7. Choose Project ▸ Prepare & Check.
  8. Choose Project ▸ Build.

Game Settings overrides the cycle 1 factors and its three carry switches until you reset those fields with their x buttons. See save compatibility for recovery and carry details.

Add extras#

Extras ships off. Once something is unlocked, the title screen shows EXTRAS with a Jukebox of music tracks, a Gallery of images and Credits that replay each ending the player has seen. Unlocks belong to this game's profile, so they stay after a save slot is deleted. A Dev Menu test session never unlocks anything.

To turn Extras on:

  1. Choose Project ▸ Game Settings….
  2. Click Ending.
  3. Tick Extras ▸ Enabled.
  4. Click Save and Prepare & Check.
  5. Choose Project ▸ Build.

Credits need no list: each ending the player reaches adds a row. To add jukebox tracks:

  1. Open developer/config/extras.lua.
  2. Add one entry to jukebox.tracks for each track, with track set to a music track id from developer/assets/music/ and title set to the name players see.
  3. Leave out unlock to unlock a track once it has played in any save slot, or set another unlock type from the list at the top of the file.
  4. Save the file.
  5. Choose Project ▸ Reload Editor Scripts.
  6. Choose Project ▸ Prepare & Check.
  7. Choose Project ▸ Build.

To add gallery images:

  1. Copy each image, as a .png file, into developer/assets/extras/. Name it with letters, digits, _ or - only, for example concept_1.png.
  2. Open developer/config/extras.lua.
  3. Add one entry to gallery.entries for each image, with id set to a unique name, image set to the file name without .png, title set to the name players see and caption set to an optional line shown under the image.
  4. Set unlock to one of the types listed at the top of the file. An entry without unlock is always unlocked.
  5. Save the file.
  6. Choose Project ▸ Reload Editor Scripts.
  7. Choose Project ▸ Prepare & Check.
  8. Choose Project ▸ Build.

To check them:

  1. Play an empty save slot to an ending.
  2. Return to the title screen.
  3. Choose EXTRAS.
  4. Choose a track on the Jukebox tab. It plays at once; Stop music stops it.
  5. Press E, or the right shoulder button on a controller, to open Gallery.
  6. Choose an image. It fills the screen; Left and Right show the other unlocked images.
  7. Press Backspace, or the right face button on a controller, to return to the list.
  8. Press E to open Credits.
  9. Choose the ending. Its title, text and credits pages play without the statistics line.
  10. Press Backspace, or the right face button on a controller, to return to the list.
  11. Press Escape to return to the title.

A track with a blank title shows as Track and its number. With Extras ▸ Show locked entries ticked, locked tracks show as ???; untick it to hide them. A gallery entry with a flag unlock shows a notice when the flag is set, with Extras ▸ Unlock notices ticked. Prepare & Check stops when an image in developer/assets/extras/ is not a readable PNG, has a side over 4096 pixels or has a name with other characters, and warns when the images hold more than 16 megapixels in total or about unknown tracks, images, endings or achievements in extras.lua. If you add a font or texture to developer/ui/game/completion.gui, add the same entry to developer/ui/menus/extras_viewer.gui, which draws the credits replay.

Printable First-World Checklist#

  • Defold 1.14.1 (or a later 1.14.x hotfix) and Tiled 1.12.x installed.
  • Toolkit extracted to MyGame/Metroidvania_Toolkit; maps kept beside it in MyGame/Tiled Worlds.
  • game.project opened in Defold, Project ▸ Set Up This Toolkit run, and sanity Build passed.
  • Tiled's defold and lua plugins ticked under Edit ▸ Preferences... ▸ Plugins.
  • MVTK.tiled-project opened and Tiled extensions enabled.
  • External tileset created with the real tile width/height.
  • Tile Collision Editor shapes saved in the .tsx.
  • start_room, middle_room, and goal_room created with new_room_blank.
  • Every room has environment art, bounds, and shaped floor/wall tiles on layers that do not scroll (or Solid Box/Slope objects).
  • Exactly one intended player_start placed.
  • Door markers paint right↔left connections between all three rooms.
  • All rooms and tilesets saved and exported.
  • Reload Editor Scripts completed.
  • Prepare & Check completed without errors.
  • Build completed.
  • Game Settings tuned with the game stopped after imported art established tile size; Save and Prepare & Check succeeded, then the game was rebuilt.
  • Layout Editor Rescan lists all three rooms.
  • Rooms snapped at matching doors.
  • Layout Editor Export completed before Menu.
  • Empty save slot opens Choose difficulty (unless fixed difficulty is configured).
  • Selecting Normal starts in start_room and reaches goal_room.

17. Maintainer Appendix#

This section is for toolkit maintainers and advanced diagnosis. First-time creators can stop above.

Architecture and ownership#

  • Tiled .tmx/.tsx plus source art are authoring truth.
  • tiled/extensions/export_lua_and_defold.js owns Tiled export/localization.
  • hooks/editor_commands.lua registers the Defold editor commands.
  • Prepare & Check owns tilesource/tilemap preparation and generated tile settings.
  • Prepare & Check owns baked room collections/factories and invokes enemy/music sync.
  • mvtk/editor/layout_editor/ owns in-game room placement UX.
  • Layout Editor Export owns the published runtime layout/minimap artifacts.
  • mvtk/rooms/runtime_layout_loader.lua prefers the published local runtime layout.

Current editor command surface#

The toolkit's Project commands are:

  • Prepare & Check
  • Use Tiled World Layout
  • Preparation Status
  • Set Up This Toolkit
  • Game Settings…
  • Dialogue Editor…
  • Loot Tables…
  • Translations…
  • Spine Add-on…
  • Steam Add-on…
  • Analyze Progression
  • Export Game
  • Reset Project to Empty (destructive)

There is no separate room-generation shortcut in the Assets context menu. Individual maintenance stages remain available to repository automation through get_maintenance_commands(); creator workflows use Prepare & Check.

Use the optional Steam add-on: it ships off. See the Steam add-on guide for achievement mapping, rich presence and Steam Cloud setup through Project ▸ Steam Add-on….

Validation commands#

Prepare & Check runs the project checks for you. To repeat two of them outside the editor while diagnosing a failure, run these from the toolkit folder with Lua 5.1 installed (the Tiled export check needs at least one exported room):

lua tools/validate_config.lua
lua tools/validate_tiled_export.lua --quick

The generated catalogs (tiled/templates/README.html and developer/config/INDEX.md) are produced from the live templates and config files; do not hand-edit them. The automated test suite, its fixture project and the documentation generators live in the toolkit's source repository and are not part of this download.

Generated from docs/CREATOR_WORKFLOW.md. The live Tiled template catalog remains generated from the shipped .tx files.
